C3a code is LOCKED post-S7b SHIP. S8 is purely\n           additive (new test files, possibly new UI files per Q1\n           outcome). Refactoring would violate Pattern A (fix-as-\n           bandage if there's a real issue, scope-creep otherwise).\n\n       (f) \"Add an end-to-end auth-bypass tooling layer\" \u2014 PUSH\n           BACK. Admin token in test environment is sufficient;\n           an explicit bypass mechanism is a security-anti-pattern\n           and never desirable.\n\n       (g) \"Use Pydantic / OpenAPI / JSON Schema validators on\n           response shapes\" \u2014 PUSH BACK. Tests that hardcode the\n           shape are direct, readable, and catch shape drift the\n           same way. Adding a contract DSL adds surface area for\n           drift between schema and code.\n\n       (h) \"Add visual regression testing (Percy, Chromatic) if\n           UI is in scope\" \u2014 PUSH BACK to a later session. Even\n           under reading-(a), v0.1 doesn't lock in visual\n           regression as part of validation suite.\n\n       (i) \"Run tests against a real Railway preview environment\"\n           \u2014 PUSH BACK as default. Smoke tests (already shipped\n           Session 26 in `smoke_c3a_deploy.py`) cover this. S8\n           tests run against in-process fixtures.\n\n       New pushbacks may emerge from critique rounds; capture\n       them in this section as they do.\n\n\u00a7 0.4  Open questions inventory (resolved in critique rounds):\n\n       **Q1 (BLOCKING \u2014 Ramalingam adjudicates before round 1):**\n       Does S8 include building the C3a UI surfaces, or is S8\n       API-level integration tests + a written contract for a\n       future UI?\n\n       Two readings, both spec-defensible:\n\n       Reading (b) \u2014 minimal \u2014 picked for v0.1 DRAFT:\n         S8 = API-level validation only. End-to-end test cases\n         that exercise the full C3a state machine via HTTP calls\n         from a test fixture against a fresh server (in-process\n         or subprocess). Plus a written UI-contract document\n         capturing the request/response shapes a future UI\n         consumer must satisfy. ~1 week of work. Path to C3a\n         SHIP.\n\n       Reading (a) \u2014 maximal:\n         S8 = reading (b) PLUS building the C3a UI surfaces.\n         Five HTML/JS/CSS pages: question-and-options view,\n         terminal-success view, abort-confirmation view, cba-\n         checklist view, fallback-resume view. Plus playwright\n         (or similar) browser-driven integration tests against\n         them. ~3 weeks of work. Defers C3a SHIP by a session.\n\n       The product-strategy question this answers: does C3a SHIP\n       require a UI, or does the UI live in a separate component\n       (e.g., a hypothetical \"UX-Surfaces\" component covering\n       all customer-facing forms)?\n\n       v0.1 DRAFT picks (b) deliberately \u2014 it's the smallest\n       complete interpretation that satisfies the \"validation\n       suite\" framing. Reading (a) is a clean SUPERSET of\n       reading (b), so spec sections below are written assuming\n       (b) and the maximal-scope additions are flagged inline as\n       `[Q1-(a) ADDS:]` notes.\n\n       Q2 \u2014 Does S8 produce a DEPLOY.md document, or just spec\n       sections that go into S7b's existing DEPLOY.md? Author\n       position: just spec sections. No new top-level DEPLOY.md.\n       This is open for round 1 critique.\n\n       Q3 \u2014 Does S8 cover the brief\u2192C3a integration surface (C1\n       producing brief tokens that C3a consumes)? Author position:\n       NO \u2014 that's a C1\u00d7C3a integration test, not C3a internal\n       validation. S8 stops at the C3a entry boundary; tests\n       seed brief_token rows directly into gate_states fixtures.\n       This is open for round 1 critique.\n\n       Q4 \u2014 Smoke tests already exist (`smoke_c3a_deploy.py`,\n       Session 26). Does S8 expand them, replace them, or leave\n       them alone? Author position: leave alone. S8 in-process\n       integration tests are the \"test suite\" half; smoke tests\n       are the \"post-deploy verification\" half. Both surface\n       coexist.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 1. PURPOSE & SCOPE\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 1.1  S8 owns ONE deliverable, in this version of the draft:\n\n       1. **Validation suite** \u2014 A new test directory\n          `tests/validation/` containing end-to-end integration\n          tests that exercise the full C3a state machine through\n          its HTTP surface. Each test runs against a freshly-\n          initialized in-process HTTP server with isolated\n          fixtures (temp DB, mocked email hook, controllable\n          clock). Coverage targets are enumerated in \u00a7 4.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       2. **C3a UI surfaces** \u2014 Five HTML/JS/CSS pages under\n          `static/c3a/` rendering the C3a state machine for the\n          end-user.\n       3. **Browser-driven integration tests** \u2014 playwright tests\n          under `tests/e2e/` exercising those UI surfaces against\n          a running server.\n\n\u00a7 1.2  Locked-surface discipline.\n\n       (i) S7b SPEC v1.0 LOCKED is invariant. S8 does NOT amend\n           any S7b invariant (P19\u2013P35). If S8 needs behavior\n           outside the S7b surface, that's a spec amendment which\n           should be handled separately \u2014 NOT folded into S8.\n\n       (ii) Already-shipped C3a code (S1\u2013S6, B-027, S7a, S7b) is\n            LOCKED. S8 is additive only:\n            - NEW test files under `tests/validation/`\n            - [Q1-(a) ADDS:] NEW `static/c3a/` files\n            - [Q1-(a) ADDS:] NEW `tests/e2e/` files\n            - NEW spec section (this document) attached to the\n              spec chain\n            - NEW changelog entries in this document only\n\n       (iii) No production code is modified by S8 unless a\n             critique round surfaces a one-line additive\n             omission (analogous to the Session-26 truncation\n             patch). Such patches are ratified by an entry in\n             the spec changelog and tagged PATCH-IN-S8-SCOPE.\n\n\u00a7 1.3  What S8 does NOT cover.\n\n       (a) Performance benchmarks. (Separate session.)\n       (b) Multi-tenant isolation. (No multi-tenancy in v1.)\n       (c) Cross-component (C1\u00d7C3a, C2\u00d7C3a) integration tests.\n           (Owned by separate spec when those integrations are\n           operationalized.)\n       (d) Security audit. (Owned by a future security-pass spec.)\n       (e) Production deployment to a particular target. (Owned\n           by DEPLOY.md, not S8.)\n       (f) Backlog items B-040 through B-053. Each has its own\n           trigger and queue position; S8 does NOT consume\n           backlog work.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 2. INVARIANTS (NON-NEGOTIABLE)\n================================================================\n\nS8 introduces a small number of new invariants (P36\u2013P39) that\nextend the S7b chain. None modify S7b's P19\u2013P35.\n\n\u00a7 2.1  P36 \u2014 Validation tests are deterministic.\n\n       Every test in `tests/validation/` produces the same\n       result on every run, in every order, on every machine\n       running Python 3.10+ with the project's pinned deps.\n\n       Mechanism:\n       - Time is controlled via a `FrozenClock` fixture\n         (parameter to fixture builders that makes time.time()\n         and int(time.time()) return deterministic values).\n       - Random-token generators are seeded for the test session.\n       - SQLite uses `:memory:` or `tempfile` (no shared state\n         between tests).\n       - HTTP responses don't depend on real network (Resend\n         hook is mocked via `unittest.mock.patch` per S7b \u00a7 8.6).\n\n       Verification: re-run the suite 10 times in random order;\n       ZERO flakes acceptable. Captured as a CI requirement,\n       not a test (running 10x in CI = same suite, would just\n       cost 10x).\n\n\u00a7 2.2  P37 \u2014 Validation tests run in under 60 seconds total.\n\n       The full `tests/validation/` suite executes in \u2264 60 sec\n       on a developer laptop (single-process, SQLite in tempfile).\n       Rationale: integration tests that take longer get skipped\n       or run-eventually, defeating their purpose.\n\n       If P37 is at risk, address by:\n       - Sharing a single in-process HTTP server across tests\n         in the same module (setUpClass / setUpModule).\n       - Using `:memory:` SQLite where the test doesn't need\n         persistence across in-process actors.\n       - NOT by parallelizing test execution. Determinism\n         (P36) > speed.\n\n\u00a7 2.3  P38 \u2014 Validation tests are HERMETIC.\n\n       Test execution does not require:\n       - Network access\n       - A running Resend account\n       - A specific machine clock value\n       - Environment variables beyond what the test sets itself\n         (in particular: NO RESEND_API_KEY required; NO\n         BUILDEMUP_DATABASE_PATH required)\n       - File-system locations outside tempfile.gettempdir()\n\n       This invariant is what makes P36 and P37 achievable.\n       Hermetic = reproducible = can run anywhere.\n\n\u00a7 2.4  P39 \u2014 Validation tests document the C3a contract.\n\n       Each test class has a docstring naming the invariant or\n       behavior under test in spec terms. A reader who finds\n       a test failure can identify which P-invariant or which\n       \u00a7 section is being exercised without reading the test\n       body.\n\n       Operational benefit: when production behavior drifts\n       from spec, the failing test names which contract is\n       being violated.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       \u00a7 2.5  P40 \u2014 UI tests exercise UI through a real browser\n              engine, not via DOM-mock libraries.\n       \u00a7 2.6  P41 \u2014 UI tests use semantic selectors (role, label,\n              text) NOT brittle CSS selectors. Tests should\n              survive cosmetic CSS changes.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 3. ARCHITECTURE\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 3.1  Test directory layout (S8 adds):\n\n       ```\n       buildemup/\n         tests/\n           validation/                   [NEW S8]\n             __init__.py\n             conftest.py                 -- shared fixtures\n             test_c3a_state_machine.py   -- happy-path traversal\n             test_c3a_idempotency.py     -- P12 + P21 + P28\n             test_c3a_termination.py     -- 5 terminal states\n             test_c3a_24h_fallback.py    -- scheduler chain\n             test_c3a_admin_recovery.py  -- admin reset paths\n             test_c3a_observability.py   -- /health + metrics\n             test_c3a_failure_modes.py   -- 503 / 4xx / 5xx\n       ```\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       ```\n         tests/\n           e2e/                          [NEW S8 reading-(a)]\n             __init__.py\n             playwright.config.json\n             test_c3a_browser_flow.py\n             test_c3a_browser_resume.py\n             test_c3a_browser_abort.py\n         static/\n           c3a/                          [NEW S8 reading-(a)]\n             question_options.html\n             question_options.js\n             question_options.css\n             terminal_success.html\n             abort_confirm.html\n             cba_checklist.html\n             fallback_resume.html\n       ```\n\n\u00a7 3.2  Fixture architecture.\n\n       Tests use a layered fixture model:\n\n       (a) `temp_db_path` (function-scoped) \u2014 produces a unique\n           SQLite path under tempfile.gettempdir(). Cleanup on\n           teardown.\n\n       (b) `fresh_storage(temp_db_path)` \u2014 initializes\n           GateStateStorage and SchedulerStateStorage against\n           that path, with `_ensure_schema` applied. Returns a\n           tuple. Resets the singleton injection points in the\n           api modules so tests don't leak storage between runs.\n\n       (c) `frozen_clock(t0=...)` \u2014 patches time.time and\n           datetime.utcnow to return controllable values. Tests\n           manipulate the clock with `frozen_clock.advance(secs)`\n           rather than time.sleep. Required for the 24h fallback\n           test (which would otherwise run for 24h).\n\n       (d) `live_server(fresh_storage, frozen_clock)` \u2014 spins up\n           an in-process HTTPServer thread on a free port. Yields\n           a base URL. Teardown does srv.shutdown() + thread.join.\n\n       (e) `mocked_resend(live_server)` \u2014 patches urllib.request\n           so the email hook records calls without sending.\n\n       (f) `seeded_brief(fresh_storage, brief_token=..., ...)` \u2014\n           inserts a `gate_states` row representing a\n           previously-captured brief, so c3a tests start from a\n           valid C1-output state without coupling to C1.\n\n       Fixtures live in `tests/validation/conftest.py`. pytest's\n       fixture model is the host (we already use pytest project-\n       wide).\n\n\u00a7 3.3  Test-suite seams (what's mocked vs real):\n\n       REAL:\n       - SchedulerStateStorage and GateStateStorage (against temp\n         SQLite) \u2014 these ARE under test.\n       - All five c3a_endpoint handlers \u2014 under test.\n       - admin_endpoint, health_endpoint, server.py \u2014 under test.\n       - JSON parsing, HTTP framing, content-length, CORS headers.\n\n       MOCKED:\n       - Resend email hook (urllib.request.urlopen). Test\n         captures the constructed Request to assert on shape;\n         the hook's action is recorded but no network call.\n       - `time.time()` and `datetime.utcnow()` in tests that\n         exercise time-window logic.\n\n       NEITHER REAL NOR MOCKED (i.e., not exercised at all):\n       - C1 (brief capture). Tests seed brief_token rows into\n         gate_states directly via fixture.\n       - C2 (feasibility). Out of S8 scope.\n       - The Resend service. Hook is mocked; nothing reaches\n         the real Resend.\n\n\u00a7 3.4  Test data strategy.\n\n       (a) Brief tokens are deterministic UUIDs based on test\n           name (e.g., `brief-test_happy_path-001`) so failures\n           are debuggable.\n       (b) Session tokens come from the real handler responses\n           (not synthesized) so the format-version chain stays\n           realistic.\n       (c) Fixture brief_token data uses minimal valid JSON\n           shapes \u2014 just enough to satisfy gate_state validators.\n       (d) No real PII in test data. Email addresses are of the\n           form `test+<test_name>@example.test` (a TLD reserved\n           by RFC 2606 for testing).\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 4. TEST PLAN\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 4.1  Test count target.\n\n       v0.1 DRAFT estimate: **~50 new validation tests** across\n       7 files. Final count emerges from critique rounds.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:] +~15 e2e tests in `tests/e2e/`.\n\n\u00a7 4.2  test_c3a_state_machine.py (~10 tests).\n\n       Walks the C3a state machine end-to-end via HTTP:\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 cba-verified \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 (24h elapsed via frozen_clock) \u2192 admin tick \u2192\n         cba-fallback-continue invoked \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 abort \u2192 USER_ABORTED\n       - Various option-id permutations\n       - State transitions verified via direct gate_states reads\n\n\u00a7 4.3  test_c3a_idempotency.py (~7 tests).\n\n       P12, P21, P28 verified end-to-end:\n       - Same brief_token + request_id on /check returns same\n         session_token (P12 uniqueness).\n       - Same session_token + request_id on /resolve returns\n         cached response (P21 idempotency).\n       - Two parallel admin/scheduler/tick calls claim distinct\n         rows from scheduler_state (P28 atomic dequeue).\n       - Same request_id on cba-fallback-continue returns cached\n         response.\n       - Idempotency across server restart (DB persistence).\n\n\u00a7 4.4  test_c3a_termination.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Each of the 5 terminal states reachable + properly\n       finalized:\n       - TERMINAL_OK\n       - USER_ABORTED\n       - CBA_VERIFIED\n       - CBA_FALLBACK_FIRED\n       - SCHEMA_DEPRECATED (shape only \u2014 synthetic)\n       - is_terminal=1 set in DB\n       - Subsequent operations on terminal sessions return\n         410 Gone with code `terminal`\n\n\u00a7 4.5  test_c3a_24h_fallback.py (~8 tests).\n\n       The full P21 + P33 24h-fallback chain:\n       - check \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED enqueues a\n         scheduler_state row with fire_at = now + 86400.\n       - frozen_clock.advance(86400 + 60).\n       - admin/scheduler/tick claims it, posts to\n         /cba-fallback-continue, commits fired.\n       - Failed POST \u2192 record_error \u2192 re-claim after cooldown.\n       - 3 failed POSTs \u2192 row stuck (P33), surfaced via /health.\n       - admin/scheduler/reset clears stuck row.\n\n\u00a7 4.6  test_c3a_admin_recovery.py (~5 tests).\n\n       P35 admin reset paths exhaustively:\n       - RESET outcome on stuck row.\n       - ALREADY_FIRED on committed row.\n       - NOT_FOUND on unknown token.\n       - Auth failure: missing token \u2192 401.\n       - Auth failure: wrong token \u2192 401.\n\n\u00a7 4.7  test_c3a_observability.py (~6 tests).\n\n       /health + metrics surface:\n       - GET /health returns 200 + diagnostics in healthy state.\n       - GET /health returns 503 + code on failed write check.\n       - scheduler_due_count + scheduler_stuck_count reflect\n         real queue state.\n       - Each handler emits exactly one structured-log metric\n         line per call.\n       - Metric line schema is exactly the closed P30 vocabulary.\n       - Trace_id propagated end-to-end through metric lines.\n\n\u00a7 4.8  test_c3a_failure_modes.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Failure paths produce correct HTTP behavior:\n       - SQLite \"database is locked\" \u2192 503 + storage_busy +\n         Retry-After: 5.\n       - Oversized request body \u2192 400 + request_too_large.\n       - Malformed JSON \u2192 400 + bad_json.\n       - Missing required field \u2192 400 + validation.\n       - Unknown session_token \u2192 404 + unknown_token.\n       - Action on terminal session \u2192 410 + terminal.\n       - X-Trace-Id malformed \u2192 fresh trace_id + WARNING log.\n\n\u00a7 4.9  Test invocation.\n\n       From repo root:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/validation/ -v\n       ```\n\n       Subset:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/validation/test_c3a_24h_fallback.py\n       ```\n\n       Run as part of the full suite:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/ -v\n       ```\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       For e2e:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/e2e/ -v\n       ```\n       Requires `playwright install chromium` once locally.\n       In CI, runs against a Railway preview deploy.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 5. CONTRACT DOCUMENT FOR FUTURE UI\n================================================================\n\n[This section is the \"UI \u2190\u2192 API contract\" half of S8 reading-(b).\nUnder reading-(a), this section becomes the implementation guide\nfor the UI surfaces themselves.]\n\n\u00a7 5.1  Endpoints C3a exposes for UI consumption:\n\n       GET  /health\n       POST /api/extreme-case/check\n       POST /api/extreme-case/resolve\n       POST /api/extreme-case/abort\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-fallback-continue\n            (USED BY THE SCHEDULER, NOT THE UI)\n\n       GET  /api/brief/resume?token=\u2026  (used to load a\n            previously-saved brief; standard C1 endpoint, listed\n            here because the cba-checklist resume flow uses it)\n\n\u00a7 5.2  Request/response shapes.\n\n       Each endpoint's shape is fully specified in the c3a_endpoint\n       module docstrings + S7a SPEC \u00a7 5. The UI contract is: those\n       shapes are the source of truth and won't change without a\n       new C3a sub-session spec.\n\n       Headers the UI MUST send:\n       - Content-Type: application/json on POSTs.\n       - X-Trace-Id: <16-hex> (optional but recommended for\n         debug/support correlation).\n\n       Headers the UI MUST handle:\n       - Retry-After (on 503 storage_busy responses).\n       - Standard CORS headers (Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *\n         is set by server.py).\n\n\u00a7 5.3  Status code semantics for UI consumers:\n\n       200 \u2014 success. Body always JSON object with `ok: true`.\n       400 \u2014 validation. Body has `errors: [{code, message}]`.\n       401 \u2014 auth. Admin endpoints only; UI shouldn't see this.\n       404 \u2014 unknown_token. Show \"session not found\" UX.\n       409 \u2014 already_fired (admin reset only).\n       410 \u2014 terminal. Show \"this case is closed\" UX.\n       503 \u2014 storage_busy. Honor Retry-After; show transient\n             error UX.\n\n\u00a7 5.4  Trace_id propagation expectation.\n\n       UI generates a 16-hex trace_id at the start of a user\n       session, sends it on every C3a call, and surfaces it in\n       any error UI (\"if you contact support, share this code:\n       <trace_id>\").\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:] Implemented in shared `static/c3a/_trace.js`.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 6. REQUEST-RESPONSE MODEL (UI flows)\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 6.1  Happy path:\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/check {brief_token, request_id}\n           \u2190 200 {session_token, trace_id, extreme_case: {options}}\n       UI shows the case + options\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve {session_token,\n                                            request_id, option_id}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal, terminal_state, trace_id}\n       UI shows success.\n\n\u00a7 6.2  Pause-and-continue path (24h):\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve {\u2026, requires_cba}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: false, paused_state: \n                  CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED, cba_checklist_url, \n                  trace_id}\n       UI shows the cba-checklist page with link to come back.\n       (User leaves; closes browser; opens email N hours later.)\n       UI \u2190 email link \u2192 load checklist page via GET /api/brief/\n                                                   resume?token=\u2026\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified {session_token,\n                                                 request_id, \n                                                 verified: true}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: true, terminal_state: \n                  CBA_VERIFIED, trace_id}\n       UI shows success.\n\n\u00a7 6.3  Abort path:\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/abort {session_token, \n                                          request_id}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: true, terminal_state: \n                  USER_ABORTED, trace_id}\n       UI shows abort confirmation.\n\n\u00a7 6.4  Failure handling:\n\n       On 503 + Retry-After:\n         UI displays \"checking...\", waits Retry-After seconds,\n         re-sends the SAME request (with the SAME request_id).\n         P21 idempotency guarantees no duplicate side-effects.\n\n       On 4xx other than 503:\n         UI displays the error message from\n         `body.errors[0].message`. Includes `body.trace_id` if\n         present. Does NOT auto-retry (4xx is client-side; auto-\n         retry would just amplify the error).\n\n       On 5xx other than 503:\n         UI displays a generic \"system error\" with trace_id.\n         Logs to console for debug.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 7. EXECUTION & TIMING\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 7.1  Test wall-clock budget: 60 seconds total (P37). \n\n       Distributed roughly:\n       - state_machine ~10s\n       - idempotency ~5s\n       - termination ~5s\n       - 24h_fallback ~15s (the bulk; multiple full tick cycles)\n       - admin_recovery ~5s\n       - observability ~10s\n       - failure_modes ~5s\n       - fixture init/teardown overhead ~5s\n\n\u00a7 7.2  CI integration.\n\n       Validation tests run in the same pytest invocation as\n       the existing project-wide tests. NO separate test phase.\n       This is enforced by the standard pytest discovery rules.\n\n       After S8 ships:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest buildemup/tests -q\n       # \u2192 expected: 1182 + ~50 = ~1232 passed\n       ```\n\n\u00a7 7.3  Smoke test interaction.\n\n       `smoke_c3a_deploy.py` (shipped Session 26) is unchanged.\n       It's the post-deploy verification surface; this validation\n       suite is the in-process pre-deploy verification surface.\n       Both coexist without overlap.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 8. SCHEMA & ROLLOUT\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 8.1  No schema changes.\n\n       S8 is purely additive in tests + (under reading-a) static\n       files. No changes to:\n       - gate_states schema\n       - scheduler_state schema\n       - any production code module\n       - any environment variable\n       - any HTTP endpoint shape\n\n\u00a7 8.2  Rollback strategy.\n\n       S8 ships when validation suite goes green and Ramalingam\n       confirms. If a critical issue surfaces post-S8-ship, the\n       rollback is to revert the validation-tests directory\n       commit. Production code is untouched, so production is\n       unaffected.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:] Under reading-(a), the C3a UI is part of\n       the \"shipped\" surface. Rollback is harder because customers\n       see the UI; would need a feature-flag or deploy-rollback\n       at Railway.\n\n\u00a7 8.3  Backward compatibility.\n\n       Trivially preserved (additive only).\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 9. OPEN QUESTIONS (deploy-time / housekeeping \u2014\n       NOT spec-level blockers)\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 9.1  Q5 \u2014 Should the validation suite include a test verifying\n       the B-050 / B-051 deploy-time concerns? Author position: NO.\n       Those are deploy-time concerns, not validation-suite\n       concerns. They belong in DEPLOY.md verification (likely\n       smoke-test extensions). Open for round 1 critique.\n\n\u00a7 9.2  Q6 \u2014 Should validation tests use pytest fixtures, or\n       unittest TestCase subclasses (as Phase 9 did)? Author\n       position: pytest fixtures, because the project uses pytest\n       discovery and conftest.py is the right home for shared\n       fixtures. S8 stops at the C3a entry boundary; tests\n       seed brief_token rows directly into gate_states fixtures.\n       This is open for round 1 critique.\n\n       Q4 \u2014 Smoke tests already exist (`smoke_c3a_deploy.py`,\n       Session 26). S8 in-process\n       integration tests are the \"test suite\" half; smoke tests\n       are the \"post-deploy verification\" half. Both surface\n       coexist.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 1. PURPOSE & SCOPE\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 1.1  S8 owns ONE deliverable, in this version of the draft:\n\n       1. **Validation suite** \u2014 A new test directory\n          `tests/validation/` containing end-to-end integration\n          tests that exercise the full C3a state machine through\n          its HTTP surface. Each test runs against a freshly-\n          initialized in-process HTTP server with isolated\n          fixtures (temp DB, mocked email hook, controllable\n          clock). Coverage targets are enumerated in \u00a7 4.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       2. **C3a UI surfaces** \u2014 Five HTML/JS/CSS pages under\n          `static/c3a/` rendering the C3a state machine for the\n          end-user.\n       3. **Browser-driven integration tests** \u2014 playwright tests\n          under `tests/e2e/` exercising those UI surfaces against\n          a running server.\n\n\u00a7 1.2  Locked-surface discipline.\n\n       (i) S7b SPEC v1.0 LOCKED is invariant. S8 does NOT amend\n           any S7b invariant (P19\u2013P35). If S8 needs behavior\n           outside the S7b surface, that's a spec amendment which\n           should be handled separately \u2014 NOT folded into S8.\n\n       (ii) Already-shipped C3a code (S1\u2013S6, B-027, S7a, S7b) is\n            LOCKED. S8 is additive only:\n            - NEW test files under `tests/validation/`\n            - [Q1-(a) ADDS:] NEW `static/c3a/` files\n            - [Q1-(a) ADDS:] NEW `tests/e2e/` files\n            - NEW spec section (this document) attached to the\n              spec chain\n            - NEW changelog entries in this document only\n\n       (iii) No production code is modified by S8 unless a\n             critique round surfaces a one-line additive\n             omission (analogous to the Session-26 truncation\n             patch). Such patches are ratified by an entry in\n             the spec changelog and tagged PATCH-IN-S8-SCOPE.\n\n\u00a7 1.3  What S8 does NOT cover.\n\n       (a) Performance benchmarks. (Separate session.)\n       (b) Multi-tenant isolation. (No multi-tenancy in v1.)\n       (c) Cross-component (C1\u00d7C3a, C2\u00d7C3a) integration tests.\n           (Owned by separate spec when those integrations are\n           operationalized.)\n       (d) Security audit. (Owned by a future security-pass spec.)\n       (e) Production deployment to a particular target. (Owned\n           by DEPLOY.md, not S8.)\n       (f) Backlog items B-040 through B-053. Each has its own\n           trigger and queue position; S8 does NOT consume\n           backlog work.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 2. INVARIANTS (NON-NEGOTIABLE)\n================================================================\n\nS8 introduces a small number of new invariants (P36\u2013P39) that\nextend the S7b chain. None modify S7b's P19\u2013P35.\n\n\u00a7 2.1  P36 \u2014 Validation tests are deterministic.\n\n       Every test in `tests/validation/` produces the same\n       result on every run, in every order, on every machine\n       running Python 3.10+ with the project's pinned deps.\n\n       Mechanism:\n       - Time is controlled via a `FrozenClock` fixture\n         (parameter to fixture builders that makes time.time()\n         and int(time.time()) return deterministic values).\n       - Random-token generators are seeded for the test session.\n       - SQLite uses `:memory:` or `tempfile` (no shared state\n         between tests).\n       - HTTP responses don't depend on real network (Resend\n         hook is mocked via `unittest.mock.patch` per S7b \u00a7 8.6).\n\n       Verification: re-run the suite 10 times in random order;\n       ZERO flakes acceptable. Captured as a CI requirement,\n       not a test (running 10x in CI = same suite, would just\n       cost 10x).\n\n\u00a7 2.2  P37 \u2014 Validation tests run in under 60 seconds total.\n\n       The full `tests/validation/` suite executes in \u2264 60 sec\n       on a developer laptop (single-process, SQLite in tempfile).\n       Rationale: integration tests that take longer get skipped\n       or run-eventually, defeating their purpose.\n\n       If P37 is at risk, address by:\n       - Sharing a single in-process HTTP server across tests\n         in the same module (setUpClass / setUpModule).\n       - Using `:memory:` SQLite where the test doesn't need\n         persistence across in-process actors.\n       - NOT by parallelizing test execution. Determinism\n         (P36) > speed.\n\n\u00a7 2.3  P38 \u2014 Validation tests are HERMETIC.\n\n       Test execution does not require:\n       - Network access\n       - A running Resend account\n       - A specific machine clock value\n       - Environment variables beyond what the test sets itself\n         (in particular: NO RESEND_API_KEY required; NO\n         BUILDEMUP_DATABASE_PATH required)\n       - File-system locations outside tempfile.gettempdir()\n\n       This invariant is what makes P36 and P37 achievable.\n       Hermetic = reproducible = can run anywhere.\n\n\u00a7 2.4  P39 \u2014 Validation tests document the C3a contract.\n\n       Each test class has a docstring naming the invariant or\n       behavior under test in spec terms. A reader who finds\n       a test failure can identify which P-invariant or which\n       \u00a7 section is being exercised without reading the test\n       body.\n\n       Operational benefit: when production behavior drifts\n       from spec, the failing test names which contract is\n       being violated.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       \u00a7 2.5  P40 \u2014 UI tests exercise UI through a real browser\n              engine, not via DOM-mock libraries.\n       \u00a7 2.6  P41 \u2014 UI tests use semantic selectors (role, label,\n              text) NOT brittle CSS selectors. Tests should\n              survive cosmetic CSS changes.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 3. ARCHITECTURE\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 3.1  Test directory layout (S8 adds):\n\n       ```\n       buildemup/\n         tests/\n           validation/                   [NEW S8]\n             __init__.py\n             conftest.py                 -- shared fixtures\n             test_c3a_state_machine.py   -- happy-path traversal\n             test_c3a_idempotency.py     -- P12 + P21 + P28\n             test_c3a_termination.py     -- 5 terminal states\n             test_c3a_24h_fallback.py    -- scheduler chain\n             test_c3a_admin_recovery.py  -- admin reset paths\n             test_c3a_observability.py   -- /health + metrics\n             test_c3a_failure_modes.py   -- 503 / 4xx / 5xx\n       ```\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       ```\n         tests/\n           e2e/                          [NEW S8 reading-(a)]\n             __init__.py\n             playwright.config.json\n             test_c3a_browser_flow.py\n             test_c3a_browser_resume.py\n             test_c3a_browser_abort.py\n         static/\n           c3a/                          [NEW S8 reading-(a)]\n             question_options.html\n             question_options.js\n             question_options.css\n             terminal_success.html\n             abort_confirm.html\n             cba_checklist.html\n             fallback_resume.html\n       ```\n\n\u00a7 3.2  Fixture architecture.\n\n       Tests use a layered fixture model:\n\n       (a) `temp_db_path` (function-scoped) \u2014 produces a unique\n           SQLite path under tempfile.gettempdir(). Cleanup on\n           teardown.\n\n       (b) `fresh_storage(temp_db_path)` \u2014 initializes\n           GateStateStorage and SchedulerStateStorage against\n           that path, with `_ensure_schema` applied. Returns a\n           tuple. Resets the singleton injection points in the\n           api modules so tests don't leak storage between runs.\n\n       (c) `frozen_clock(t0=...)` \u2014 patches time.time and\n           datetime.utcnow to return controllable values. Tests\n           manipulate the clock with `frozen_clock.advance(secs)`\n           rather than time.sleep. Required for the 24h fallback\n           test (which would otherwise run for 24h).\n\n       (d) `live_server(fresh_storage, frozen_clock)` \u2014 spins up\n           an in-process HTTPServer thread on a free port. Yields\n           a base URL. Teardown does srv.shutdown() + thread.join.\n\n       (e) `mocked_resend(live_server)` \u2014 patches urllib.request\n           so the email hook records calls without sending.\n\n       (f) `seeded_brief(fresh_storage, brief_token=..., ...)` \u2014\n           inserts a `gate_states` row representing a\n           previously-captured brief, so c3a tests start from a\n           valid C1-output state without coupling to C1.\n\n       Fixtures live in `tests/validation/conftest.py`. pytest's\n       fixture model is the host (we already use pytest project-\n       wide).\n\n\u00a7 3.3  Test-suite seams (what's mocked vs real):\n\n       REAL:\n       - SchedulerStateStorage and GateStateStorage (against temp\n         SQLite) \u2014 these ARE under test.\n       - All five c3a_endpoint handlers \u2014 under test.\n       - admin_endpoint, health_endpoint, server.py \u2014 under test.\n       - JSON parsing, HTTP framing, content-length, CORS headers.\n\n       MOCKED:\n       - Resend email hook (urllib.request.urlopen). Test\n         captures the constructed Request to assert on shape;\n         the hook's action is recorded but no network call.\n       - `time.time()` and `datetime.utcnow()` in tests that\n         exercise time-window logic.\n\n       NEITHER REAL NOR MOCKED (i.e., not exercised at all):\n       - C1 (brief capture). Tests seed brief_token rows into\n         gate_states directly via fixture.\n       - C2 (feasibility). Out of S8 scope.\n       - The Resend service. Hook is mocked; nothing reaches\n         the real Resend.\n\n\u00a7 3.4  Test data strategy.\n\n       (a) Brief tokens are deterministic UUIDs based on test\n           name (e.g., `brief-test_happy_path-001`) so failures\n           are debuggable.\n       (b) Session tokens come from the real handler responses\n           (not synthesized) so the format-version chain stays\n           realistic.\n       (c) Fixture brief_token data uses minimal valid JSON\n           shapes \u2014 just enough to satisfy gate_state validators.\n       (d) No real PII in test data. Email addresses are of the\n           form `test+<test_name>@example.test` (a TLD reserved\n           by RFC 2606 for testing).\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 4. TEST PLAN\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 4.1  Test count target.\n\n       v0.1 DRAFT estimate: **~50 new validation tests** across\n       7 files. Final count emerges from critique rounds.\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:] +~15 e2e tests in `tests/e2e/`.\n\n\u00a7 4.2  test_c3a_state_machine.py (~10 tests).\n\n       Walks the C3a state machine end-to-end via HTTP:\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 cba-verified \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 (24h elapsed via frozen_clock) \u2192 admin tick \u2192\n         cba-fallback-continue invoked \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 abort \u2192 USER_ABORTED\n       - Various option-id permutations\n       - State transitions verified via direct gate_states reads\n\n\u00a7 4.3  test_c3a_idempotency.py (~7 tests).\n\n       P12, P21, P28 verified end-to-end:\n       - Same brief_token + request_id on /check returns same\n         session_token (P12 uniqueness).\n       - Same session_token + request_id on /resolve returns\n         cached response (P21 idempotency).\n       - Two parallel admin/scheduler/tick calls claim distinct\n         rows from scheduler_state (P28 atomic dequeue).\n       - Same request_id on cba-fallback-continue returns cached\n         response.\n       - Idempotency across server restart (DB persistence).\n\n\u00a7 4.4  test_c3a_termination.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Each of the 5 terminal states reachable + properly\n       finalized:\n       - TERMINAL_OK\n       - USER_ABORTED\n       - CBA_VERIFIED\n       - CBA_FALLBACK_FIRED\n       - SCHEMA_DEPRECATED (shape only \u2014 synthetic)\n       - is_terminal=1 set in DB\n       - Subsequent operations on terminal sessions return\n         410 Gone with code `terminal`\n\n\u00a7 4.5  test_c3a_24h_fallback.py (~8 tests).\n\n       The full P21 + P33 24h-fallback chain:\n       - check \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED enqueues a\n         scheduler_state row with fire_at = now + 86400.\n       - frozen_clock.advance(86400 + 60).\n       - admin/scheduler/tick claims it, posts to\n         /cba-fallback-continue, commits fired.\n       - Failed POST \u2192 record_error \u2192 re-claim after cooldown.\n       - 3 failed POSTs \u2192 row stuck (P33), surfaced via /health.\n       - admin/scheduler/reset clears stuck row.\n\n\u00a7 4.6  test_c3a_admin_recovery.py (~5 tests).\n\n       P35 admin reset paths exhaustively:\n       - RESET outcome on stuck row.\n       - ALREADY_FIRED on committed row.\n       - NOT_FOUND on unknown token.\n       - Auth failure: missing token \u2192 401.\n       - Auth failure: wrong token \u2192 401.\n\n\u00a7 4.7  test_c3a_observability.py (~6 tests).\n\n       /health + metrics surface:\n       - GET /health returns 200 + diagnostics in healthy state.\n       - GET /health returns 503 + code on failed write check.\n       - scheduler_due_count + scheduler_stuck_count reflect\n         real queue state.\n       - Each handler emits exactly one structured-log metric\n         line per call.\n       - Metric line schema is exactly the closed P30 vocabulary.\n       - Trace_id propagated end-to-end through metric lines.\n\n\u00a7 4.8  test_c3a_failure_modes.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Failure paths produce correct HTTP behavior:\n       - SQLite \"database is locked\" \u2192 503 + storage_busy +\n         Retry-After: 5.\n       - Oversized request body \u2192 400 + request_too_large.\n       - Malformed JSON \u2192 400 + bad_json.\n       - Missing required field \u2192 400 + validation.\n       - Unknown session_token \u2192 404 + unknown_token.\n       - Action on terminal session \u2192 410 + terminal.\n       - X-Trace-Id malformed \u2192 fresh trace_id + WARNING log.\n\n\u00a7 4.9  Test invocation.\n\n       From repo root:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/validation/ -v\n       ```\n\n       Subset:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/validation/test_c3a_24h_fallback.py\n       ```\n\n       Run as part of the full suite:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/ -v\n       ```\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:]\n       For e2e:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest tests/e2e/ -v\n       ```\n       Requires `playwright install chromium` once locally.\n       In CI, runs against a Railway preview deploy.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 5. CONTRACT DOCUMENT FOR FUTURE UI\n================================================================\n\n[This section is the \"UI \u2190\u2192 API contract\" half of S8 reading-(b).\nUnder reading-(a), this section becomes the implementation guide\nfor the UI surfaces themselves.]\n\n\u00a7 5.1  Endpoints C3a exposes for UI consumption:\n\n       GET  /health\n       POST /api/extreme-case/check\n       POST /api/extreme-case/resolve\n       POST /api/extreme-case/abort\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-fallback-continue\n            (USED BY THE SCHEDULER, NOT THE UI)\n\n       GET  /api/brief/resume?token=\u2026  (used to load a\n            previously-saved brief; standard C1 endpoint, listed\n            here because the cba-checklist resume flow uses it)\n\n\u00a7 5.2  Request/response shapes.\n\n       Each endpoint's shape is fully specified in the c3a_endpoint\n       module docstrings + S7a SPEC \u00a7 5. The UI contract is: those\n       shapes are the source of truth and won't change without a\n       new C3a sub-session spec.\n\n       Headers the UI MUST send:\n       - Content-Type: application/json on POSTs.\n       - X-Trace-Id: <16-hex> (optional but recommended for\n         debug/support correlation).\n\n       Headers the UI MUST handle:\n       - Retry-After (on 503 storage_busy responses).\n       - Standard CORS headers (Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *\n         is set by server.py).\n\n\u00a7 5.3  Status code semantics for UI consumers:\n\n       200 \u2014 success. Body always JSON object with `ok: true`.\n       400 \u2014 validation. Body has `errors: [{code, message}]`.\n       401 \u2014 auth. Admin endpoints only; UI shouldn't see this.\n       404 \u2014 unknown_token. Show \"session not found\" UX.\n       409 \u2014 already_fired (admin reset only).\n       410 \u2014 terminal. Show \"this case is closed\" UX.\n       503 \u2014 storage_busy. Honor Retry-After; show transient\n             error UX.\n\n\u00a7 5.4  Trace_id propagation expectation.\n\n       UI generates a 16-hex trace_id at the start of a user\n       session, sends it on every C3a call, and surfaces it in\n       any error UI (\"if you contact support, share this code:\n       <trace_id>\").\n\n       [Q1-(a) ADDS:] Implemented in shared `static/c3a/_trace.js`.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 6. REQUEST-RESPONSE MODEL (UI flows)\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 6.1  Happy path:\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/check {brief_token, request_id}\n           \u2190 200 {session_token, trace_id, extreme_case: {options}}\n       UI shows the case + options\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve {session_token,\n                                            request_id, option_id}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal, terminal_state, trace_id}\n       UI shows success.\n\n\u00a7 6.2  Pause-and-continue path (24h):\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve {\u2026, requires_cba}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: false, paused_state: \n                  CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED, cba_checklist_url, \n                  trace_id}\n       UI shows the cba-checklist page with link to come back.\n       (User leaves; closes browser; opens email N hours later.)\n       UI \u2190 email link \u2192 load checklist page via GET /api/brief/\n                                                   resume?token=\u2026\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified {session_token,\n                                                 request_id, \n                                                 verified: true}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: true, terminal_state: \n                  CBA_VERIFIED, trace_id}\n       UI shows success.\n\n\u00a7 6.3  Abort path:\n\n       UI \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/abort {session_token, \n                                          request_id}\n           \u2190 200 {ok, is_terminal: true, terminal_state: \n                  USER_ABORTED, trace_id}\n       UI shows abort confirmation.\n\n\u00a7 6.4  Failure handling:\n\n       On 503 + Retry-After:\n         UI displays \"checking...\", waits Retry-After seconds,\n         re-sends the SAME request (with the SAME request_id).\n         P21 idempotency guarantees no duplicate side-effects.\n\n       On 4xx other than 503:\n         UI displays the error message from\n         `body.errors[0].message`. Includes `body.trace_id` if\n         present. Does NOT auto-retry (4xx is client-side; auto-\n         retry would just amplify the error).\n\n       On 5xx other than 503:\n         UI displays a generic \"system error\" with trace_id.\n         Logs to console for debug.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 7. EXECUTION & TIMING\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 7.1  Test wall-clock budget: 60 seconds total (P37). \n\n       Distributed roughly:\n       - state_machine ~10s\n       - idempotency ~5s\n       - termination ~5s\n       - 24h_fallback ~15s (the bulk; multiple full tick cycles)\n       - admin_recovery ~5s\n       - observability ~10s\n       - failure_modes ~5s\n       - fixture init/teardown overhead ~5s\n\n\u00a7 7.2  CI integration.\n\n       Validation tests run in the same pytest invocation as\n       the existing project-wide tests. NO separate test phase.\n       This is enforced by the standard pytest discovery rules.\n\n       After S8 ships:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest buildemup/tests -q\n       # \u2192 expected: 1182 + ~50 = ~1232 passed\n       ```\n\n\u00a7 7.3  Smoke test interaction.\n\n       `smoke_c3a_deploy.py` (shipped Session 26) is unchanged.\n       It's the post-deploy verification surface; this validation\n       suite is the in-process pre-deploy verification surface.\n       Both coexist without overlap.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 8. SCHEMA & ROLLOUT\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 8.1  No schema changes.\n\n       S8 is purely additive in tests + (under reading-a) static\n       files. No changes to:\n       - gate_states schema\n       - scheduler_state schema\n       - any production code module\n       - any environment variable\n       - any HTTP endpoint shape\n\n\u00a7 8.2  Rollback strategy.\n\n       S8 ships when validation suite goes green and Ramalingam\n       confirms. Rationale: integration\n       tests that take longer get skipped or run-eventually,\n       defeating their purpose.\n\n       Budget split:\n       - tests/validation/ (~50 tests): \u2264 60 sec\n       - tests/e2e/ (~15 tests, includes browser warmup): \u2264 30 sec\n\n       If P37 is at risk, address by:\n       - Sharing a single in-process HTTP server across tests\n         in the same module (setUpClass / setUpModule / pytest\n         fixture session scope).\n       - Using `:memory:` SQLite where the test doesn't need\n         persistence across in-process actors.\n       - Reusing the playwright browser context across e2e tests\n         in the same module.\n       - NOT by parallelizing test execution. Determinism (P36)\n         > speed.\n\n\u00a7 2.3  P38 \u2014 Validation tests are HERMETIC.\n\n       Test execution does not require:\n       - Network access\n       - A running Resend account\n       - A specific machine clock value\n       - Environment variables beyond what the test sets itself\n         (in particular: NO RESEND_API_KEY required; NO\n         BUILDEMUP_DATABASE_PATH required)\n       - File-system locations outside tempfile.gettempdir()\n\n       Playwright e2e tests require the chromium binary\n       (one-time `playwright install chromium` per dev machine\n       and per CI runner) but the test execution itself does not\n       need network beyond localhost.\n\n       Hermetic = reproducible = can run anywhere.\n\n\u00a7 2.4  P39 \u2014 Validation tests document the C3a contract.\n\n       Each test class has a docstring naming the invariant or\n       behavior under test in spec terms. A reader who finds a\n       test failure can identify which P-invariant or which \u00a7\n       section is being exercised without reading the test body.\n\n       Operational benefit: when production behavior drifts from\n       spec, the failing test names which contract is being\n       violated.\n\n\u00a7 2.5  P40 \u2014 UI tests exercise UI through a real browser engine.\n\n       E2E tests use Playwright + chromium against a live\n       in-process server. Worth it for the realism guarantee.\n\n\u00a7 2.6  P41 \u2014 UI tests use semantic selectors.\n\n       Selectors prefer (in order):\n       (a) ARIA role + accessible name: `page.get_by_role(\"button\",\n           name=\"Confirm\")`\n       (b) Form label: `page.get_by_label(\"Email\")`\n       (c) Visible text: `page.get_by_text(\"Resolve case\")`\n       (d) test-id attributes (data-testid=\"...\") for genuinely\n           unique non-textual elements.\n\n       Selectors do NOT use:\n       - Brittle CSS path selectors (`.container > .card:nth-child(2)`)\n       - Auto-generated class names\n       - XPath in any form\n\n       Tests survive cosmetic CSS changes by depending on\n       semantics, not layout.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 3. Cleanup on\n           teardown.\n\n       (b) `fresh_storage(temp_db_path)` \u2014 initializes\n           GateStateStorage and SchedulerStateStorage against\n           that path, with `_ensure_schema` applied. Returns a\n           tuple. Resets the singleton injection points in the\n           api modules so tests don't leak storage between runs.\n\n       (c) `frozen_clock(t0=...)` \u2014 patches time.time and\n           datetime.utcnow to return controllable values. Tests\n           manipulate the clock with `frozen_clock.advance(secs)`\n           rather than time.sleep. Required for the 24h fallback\n           test.\n\n       (d) `live_server(fresh_storage, frozen_clock)` \u2014 spins up\n           an in-process HTTPServer thread on a free port. Yields\n           a base URL. Teardown does srv.shutdown() + thread.join.\n           Module-scoped where individual tests don't need\n           isolation, function-scoped where they do.\n\n       (e) `mocked_resend(live_server)` \u2014 patches urllib.request\n           so the email hook records calls without sending.\n\n       (f) `seeded_brief(fresh_storage, brief_token=..., ...)` \u2014\n           inserts a `gate_states` row representing a previously-\n           captured brief, so c3a tests start from a valid\n           C1-output state without coupling to C1.\n\n       Fixtures live in `tests/validation/conftest.py`. pytest's\n       fixture model is the host (we already use pytest project-\n       wide).\n\n\u00a7 3.3  Fixture architecture (e2e suite).\n\n       E2E tests reuse the validation fixtures plus playwright-\n       specific ones:\n\n       (g) `browser` (session-scoped) \u2014 playwright Browser\n           instance. One per test session. Headless by default;\n           `PLAYWRIGHT_HEADLESS=false` for local debugging.\n\n       (h) `context(browser)` (function-scoped) \u2014 fresh\n           BrowserContext per test (cookies, storage isolated\n           per test).\n\n       (i) `page(context)` (function-scoped) \u2014 Playwright Page.\n\n       (j) `c3a_test_environment` \u2014 wraps fresh_storage +\n           live_server + mocked_resend + frozen_clock + browser\n           into one fixture for e2e tests so each test gets the\n           full stack.\n\n       Fixtures live in `tests/e2e/conftest.py`.\n\n\u00a7 3.4  Test seams (what's mocked vs real):\n\n       REAL:\n       - SchedulerStateStorage and GateStateStorage (against temp\n         SQLite) \u2014 these ARE under test.\n       - All five c3a_endpoint handlers \u2014 under test.\n       - admin_endpoint, health_endpoint, server.py \u2014 under test.\n       - JSON parsing, HTTP framing, content-length, CORS headers.\n       - Static file serving (under test for the new c3a routes).\n       - Playwright's chromium browser (real DOM, real CSS, real JS).\n\n       MOCKED:\n       - Resend email hook (urllib.request.urlopen). Test\n         captures the constructed Request to assert on shape;\n         the hook's action is recorded but no network call.\n       - `time.time()` and `datetime.utcnow()` in tests that\n         exercise time-window logic.\n\n       NEITHER REAL NOR MOCKED (i.e., not exercised at all):\n       - C1 (brief capture). Tests seed brief_token rows into\n         gate_states directly via fixture.\n       - C2 (feasibility). Out of S8 scope.\n       - The Resend service. Hook is mocked; nothing reaches\n         the real Resend.\n\n\u00a7 3.5  Test data strategy.\n\n       (a) Brief tokens are deterministic UUIDs based on test\n           name (e.g., `brief-test_happy_path-001`) so failures\n           are debuggable.\n       (b) Session tokens come from the real handler responses\n           (not synthesized) so the format-version chain stays\n           realistic.\n       (c) Fixture brief_token data uses minimal valid JSON\n           shapes \u2014 just enough to satisfy gate_state validators.\n       (d) No real PII in test data. Email addresses are of the\n           form `test+<test_name>@example.test` (a TLD reserved\n           by RFC 2606 for testing).\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 4. TEST PLAN \u2014 VALIDATION SUITE\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 4.1  Test count target.\n\n       v0.1.1 DRAFT estimate:\n       - tests/validation/ : ~50 tests across 7 files\n       - tests/e2e/        : ~15 tests across 5 files\n       - Total             : ~65 new tests\n\n       Final count emerges from critique rounds.\n\n\u00a7 4.2  test_c3a_state_machine.py (~10 tests).\n\n       Walks the C3a state machine end-to-end via HTTP:\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 cba-verified \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED\n         \u2192 (24h elapsed via frozen_clock) \u2192 admin tick \u2192\n         cba-fallback-continue invoked \u2192 terminal\n       - check \u2192 CASE_PENDING \u2192 abort \u2192 USER_ABORTED\n       - Various option-id permutations\n       - State transitions verified via direct gate_states reads\n\n\u00a7 4.3  test_c3a_idempotency.py (~7 tests).\n\n       P12, P21, P28 verified end-to-end:\n       - Same brief_token + request_id on /check returns same\n         session_token (P12 uniqueness).\n       - Same session_token + request_id on /resolve returns\n         cached response (P21 idempotency).\n       - Two parallel admin/scheduler/tick calls claim distinct\n         rows from scheduler_state (P28 atomic dequeue).\n       - Same request_id on cba-fallback-continue returns cached\n         response.\n       - Idempotency across server restart (DB persistence).\n\n\u00a7 4.4  test_c3a_termination.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Each of the 5 terminal states reachable + properly\n       finalized:\n       - TERMINAL_OK\n       - USER_ABORTED\n       - CBA_VERIFIED\n       - CBA_FALLBACK_FIRED\n       - SCHEMA_DEPRECATED (shape only \u2014 synthetic)\n       - is_terminal=1 set in DB\n       - Subsequent operations on terminal sessions return\n         410 Gone with code `terminal`\n\n\u00a7 4.5  test_c3a_24h_fallback.py (~8 tests).\n\n       The full P21 + P33 24h-fallback chain:\n       - check \u2192 resolve \u2192 CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED enqueues a\n         scheduler_state row with fire_at = now + 86400.\n       - frozen_clock.advance(86400 + 60).\n       - admin/scheduler/tick claims it, posts to\n         /cba-fallback-continue, commits fired.\n       - Failed POST \u2192 record_error \u2192 re-claim after cooldown.\n       - 3 failed POSTs \u2192 row stuck (P33), surfaced via /health.\n       - admin/scheduler/reset clears stuck row.\n       - Email hook called with right payload + trace_id.\n\n\u00a7 4.6  test_c3a_admin_recovery.py (~5 tests).\n\n       P35 admin reset paths exhaustively:\n       - RESET outcome on stuck row.\n       - ALREADY_FIRED on committed row.\n       - NOT_FOUND on unknown token.\n       - Auth failure: missing token \u2192 401.\n       - Auth failure: wrong token \u2192 401.\n\n\u00a7 4.7  test_c3a_observability.py (~6 tests).\n\n       /health + metrics surface:\n       - GET /health returns 200 + diagnostics in healthy state.\n       - GET /health returns 503 + code on failed write check.\n       - scheduler_due_count + scheduler_stuck_count reflect\n         real queue state.\n       - Each handler emits exactly one structured-log metric\n         line per call.\n       - Metric line schema is exactly the closed P30 vocabulary.\n       - Trace_id propagated end-to-end through metric lines.\n\n\u00a7 4.8  test_c3a_failure_modes.py (~7 tests).\n\n       Failure paths produce correct HTTP behavior:\n       - SQLite \"database is locked\" \u2192 503 + storage_busy +\n         Retry-After: 5.\n       - Oversized request body \u2192 400 + request_too_large.\n       - Malformed JSON \u2192 400 + bad_json.\n       - Missing required field \u2192 400 + validation.\n       - Unknown session_token \u2192 404 + unknown_token.\n       - Action on terminal session \u2192 410 + terminal.\n       - X-Trace-Id malformed \u2192 fresh trace_id + WARNING log.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 5. UI SURFACES\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 5.1  Pages overview.\n\n       Five pages under `static/c3a/`. Each is a discrete URL\n       (no client-side routing per Q9); browser back/forward,\n       bookmarks, deep links all work natively.\n\n       (i)   /c3a/case.html?brief=<token>\n             Initial entry point. Calls /api/extreme-case/check\n             with the brief token. Renders the surfaced extreme\n             case + options as radio choices. User selects an\n             option + clicks \"Resolve\". POSTs to\n             /api/extreme-case/resolve. Response either redirects\n             to /c3a/done (terminal) or /c3a/checklist (paused).\n\n       (ii)  /c3a/checklist.html?session=<token>\n             Renders the CBA verification checklist for a paused\n             case. Two paths: user clicks \"I have completed CBA\"\n             \u2192 POSTs to /api/extreme-case/cba-verified, redirects\n             to /c3a/done; OR user is here from an email link\n             24h+ later \u2014 same UX.\n\n       (iii) /c3a/done.html?session=<token>\n             Terminal success view. Reads is_terminal + \n             terminal_state from a small status fetch (or pulls\n             from URL state). Shows confirmation + the terminal\n             state label.\n\n       (iv)  /c3a/aborted.html?session=<token>\n             Abort confirmation. Reached when user clicks \"Abort\n             this case\" on the case page.\n\n       (v)   /c3a/_shared.css and /c3a/_shared.js (technically\n             not pages, but shared assets all 4 pages reference).\n\n\u00a7 5.2  Page-specific behavior \u2014 case.html.\n\n       On load:\n       - Read `brief` query param.\n       - POST /api/extreme-case/check {brief_token: <token>,\n         request_id: <new-uuid>}.\n       - On 200: render `extreme_case.case_text` as the prompt;\n         render `extreme_case.options` as a radio list with\n         option_id as values. Stash session_token in a hidden\n         field.\n       - On 4xx: show error UX with trace_id.\n       - On 503: show \"checking...\" with spinner; auto-retry\n         honoring Retry-After header.\n\n       On submit:\n       - \"Resolve\" button \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve\n         {session_token, option_id, request_id}.\n       - On 200 with is_terminal=true: window.location =\n         \"/c3a/done.html?session=<token>\".\n       - On 200 with is_terminal=false (paused state): redirect\n         to \"/c3a/checklist.html?session=<token>\".\n       - \"Abort\" button \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/abort\n         {session_token, request_id}; redirect to\n         /c3a/aborted.html.\n\n\u00a7 5.3  Page-specific behavior \u2014 checklist.html.\n\n       On load:\n       - Read `session` query param.\n       - Render the static CBA checklist content (questions\n         like \"have you completed structural verification?\",\n         \"load test signed off?\", etc.) \u2014 content is hardcoded\n         in the page; no API call needed for the checklist\n         items themselves.\n       - Show a single \"I confirm CBA is complete\" button.\n\n       On submit:\n       - POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified {session_token,\n         request_id, verified: true}.\n       - On 200: redirect to /c3a/done.\n       - On 410 (already terminal): show \"this case is already\n         closed\" + redirect to /c3a/done after delay.\n       - On 4xx: show error UX with trace_id.\n\n\u00a7 5.4  Page-specific behavior \u2014 done.html and aborted.html.\n\n       Both are minimal. On load, render a static success/abort\n       confirmation. Optional: small status fetch via a future\n       /api/extreme-case/status endpoint. v0.1.1 doesn't add\n       such an endpoint (Pattern E counter); pages render\n       confirmation from URL state alone.\n\n       Both pages link back to \"Start a new brief\" (= C1 form).\n\n\u00a7 5.5  Design language baseline (Q8 author position).\n\n       Match the existing brief_form.css aesthetic:\n       - System font stack (no web fonts loaded).\n       - Single-column layout, max-width ~640px centered.\n       - Form-focused: large clickable areas, clear labels,\n         visible focus states.\n       - Color: muted neutrals + one accent color for primary\n         actions. Status colors (red/orange/green) for\n         error/warning/success.\n       - No CSS framework. Hand-written ~200 LOC across all\n         pages, per S8 budget.\n       - Responsive: works on mobile via simple media queries\n         but is not mobile-optimized per \u00a7 1.3 (g).\n       - Accessibility: semantic HTML, proper labels, ARIA\n         roles where needed. No automated a11y audit per\n         \u00a7 1.3 (i).\n\n\u00a7 5.6  Shared assets \u2014 _shared.js.\n\n       Provides:\n       - `traceId()` \u2014 returns a stable 16-hex trace_id for\n         the current page-load (stored in sessionStorage so\n         it persists across the SPA-but-not-SPA flow).\n       - `c3aFetch(url, opts)` \u2014 wraps fetch() to:\n         - inject X-Trace-Id header automatically.\n         - inject Content-Type: application/json on POSTs.\n         - parse JSON response.\n         - on 503 + Retry-After: auto-retry once after\n           waiting Retry-After seconds.\n         - throw a typed error on non-2xx with body parsed.\n       - `displayError(err, traceId)` \u2014 renders an error UI\n         inside a target element. Used by all pages.\n\n\u00a7 5.7  Shared assets \u2014 _shared.css.\n\n       Provides design tokens (colors, spacing, font sizes)\n       plus baseline element styles (button, input, .card,\n       .error). All page CSS files import _shared.css.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 6. CONTRACT DOCUMENT \u2014 UI \u2190\u2192 API\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 6.1  Endpoints C3a UI consumes.\n\n       POST /api/extreme-case/check\n       POST /api/extreme-case/resolve\n       POST /api/extreme-case/abort\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified\n\n       Plus, NOT consumed by UI but mentioned for completeness:\n       POST /api/extreme-case/cba-fallback-continue\n            (USED BY THE SCHEDULER)\n\n\u00a7 6.2  Request/response shapes.\n\n       Each endpoint's shape is fully specified in the\n       c3a_endpoint module docstrings + S7a SPEC \u00a7 5. The UI\n       contract is: those shapes are the source of truth and\n       won't change without a new C3a sub-session spec.\n\n       Headers the UI MUST send:\n       - Content-Type: application/json on POSTs.\n       - X-Trace-Id: <16-hex> (always, via _shared.js\n         c3aFetch helper).\n\n       Headers the UI MUST handle:\n       - Retry-After (on 503 storage_busy responses).\n       - Standard CORS headers (Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *\n         is set by server.py).\n\n\u00a7 6.3  Status code semantics for UI consumers:\n\n       200 \u2014 success. Body always JSON object with `ok: true`.\n       400 \u2014 validation. Body has `errors: [{code, message}]`.\n       401 \u2014 auth. Admin endpoints only; UI shouldn't see this.\n       404 \u2014 unknown_token. Show \"session not found\" UX.\n       409 \u2014 already_fired (admin reset only).\n       410 \u2014 terminal. Show \"this case is closed\" UX.\n       503 \u2014 storage_busy. Honor Retry-After; show transient\n             error UX via _shared.js c3aFetch helper.\n\n\u00a7 6.4  Trace_id propagation expectation.\n\n       UI generates a 16-hex trace_id at the start of a user\n       session (via _shared.js traceId()), sends it on every\n       C3a call, and surfaces it in any error UI (\"if you\n       contact support, share this code: <trace_id>\").\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 7. REQUEST-RESPONSE FLOWS (UI flows)\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 7.1  Happy path:\n\n       1. User arrives at /c3a/case.html?brief=<token>.\n       2. case.js \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/check \u2192 200 with\n          surfaced case + options.\n       3. User selects option, clicks Resolve.\n       4. case.js \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve \u2192 200 with\n          is_terminal=true.\n       5. window.location \u2192 /c3a/done.html?session=<token>.\n\n\u00a7 7.2  Pause-and-continue path (24h):\n\n       1. User arrives at /c3a/case.html, selects option that\n          requires CBA verification.\n       2. case.js \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/resolve \u2192 200 with\n          is_terminal=false, paused_state=CBA_VERIFICATION_PAUSED.\n       3. window.location \u2192 /c3a/checklist.html?session=<token>.\n       4. User reads checklist. Closes browser without confirming.\n       5. (User receives email N hours later with link back to\n          /c3a/checklist.html?session=<token>.)\n       6. User clicks email link, returns to checklist page.\n       7. User clicks \"I confirm\".\n       8. checklist.js \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/cba-verified \u2192\n          200 with is_terminal=true.\n       9. window.location \u2192 /c3a/done.html?session=<token>.\n\n\u00a7 7.3  Abort path:\n\n       1. User arrives at /c3a/case.html, decides to abort.\n       2. User clicks Abort.\n       3. case.js \u2192 POST /api/extreme-case/abort \u2192 200 with\n          is_terminal=true, terminal_state=USER_ABORTED.\n       4. window.location \u2192 /c3a/aborted.html?session=<token>.\n\n\u00a7 7.4  Failure handling:\n\n       On 503 + Retry-After:\n         _shared.js c3aFetch waits Retry-After seconds and\n         retries the SAME request (same request_id). P21\n         idempotency guarantees no duplicate side-effects.\n         If the second attempt also fails, displayError() is\n         called.\n\n       On 4xx other than 503:\n         displayError() shows the error message from\n         body.errors[0].message. Includes body.trace_id.\n         Does NOT auto-retry (4xx is client-side; auto-retry\n         would just amplify the error).\n\n       On 5xx other than 503:\n         displayError() shows a generic \"system error\" with\n         trace_id. Logs to console for debug.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 8. EXECUTION & TIMING\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 8.1  Test wall-clock budget: 90 seconds total (P37).\n\n       Distributed roughly:\n       - state_machine ~10s\n       - idempotency ~5s\n       - termination ~5s\n       - 24h_fallback ~15s\n       - admin_recovery ~5s\n       - observability ~10s\n       - failure_modes ~5s\n       - validation fixture init/teardown overhead ~5s\n       - e2e suite (5 files, ~15 tests, browser warmup) ~30s\n\n\u00a7 8.2  CI integration.\n\n       Validation tests run in the same pytest invocation as\n       the existing project-wide tests. NO separate test phase.\n       This is enforced by the standard pytest discovery rules.\n\n       E2E tests can be skipped if `playwright` is not\n       installed in the environment (`pytest.importorskip`).\n       In CI, `playwright install chromium` is a setup step.\n\n       After S8 ships:\n       ```\n       python -m pytest buildemup/tests -q\n       # \u2192 expected: 1182 + ~65 = ~1247 passed\n       ```\n\n\u00a7 8.3  Smoke test interaction.\n\n       `smoke_c3a_deploy.py` (shipped Session 26) is unchanged.\n       It's the post-deploy verification surface; this validation\n       suite is the in-process pre-deploy verification surface.\n       Both coexist without overlap.\n\n\u00a7 8.4  Local dev workflow.\n\n       Developer iterating on UI:\n       1. Start the server: `python -m buildemup.api.server`\n       2. Navigate to http://localhost:8000/c3a/case.html?brief=...\n       3. Make changes to static/c3a/*; reload browser.\n\n       Developer iterating on validation tests:\n       1. `python -m pytest tests/validation/ -v -k <test_name>`\n\n       Developer iterating on e2e tests:\n       1. `PLAYWRIGHT_HEADLESS=false python -m pytest tests/e2e/\n          -v -k <test_name>` (browser opens for visual debug)\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 9. SCHEMA & ROLLOUT\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 9.1  No schema changes.\n\n       S8 is purely additive in tests + static files + (small,\n       mechanical) server.py route additions. No changes to:\n       - gate_states schema\n       - scheduler_state schema\n       - any production handler module\n       - any environment variable\n       - any HTTP API endpoint shape (only ADDS static-file\n         GET routes)\n\n\u00a7 9.2  Rollback strategy.\n\n       S8 ships when validation suite + e2e suite go green and\n       Ramalingam confirms. If a critical issue surfaces post-\n       S8-ship:\n\n       (a) UI bug: revert the static/c3a/ commit. API is\n           untouched. Server still serves the existing\n           brief_form pages.\n       (b) Test bug: revert the tests/validation/ or tests/e2e/\n           commit. Production code is untouched.\n       (c) Server route bug: revert the server.py addition.\n           Static C3a pages return 404 but no other routes\n           affected.\n\n       In all cases, rollback is partial and preserves\n       already-working surfaces.\n\n\u00a7 9.3  Deploy preparation.\n\n       Before S8 ships to production:\n       - Add `playwright` to dev/CI requirements.txt (NOT\n         production requirements; UI runs in user browsers).\n       - Add `playwright install chromium` to CI setup script.\n       - Update DEPLOY.md to mention the new C3a UI URLs (e.g.,\n         \"/c3a/case.html?brief=<token> is the entry point after\n         C1 brief capture completes\").\n\n\u00a7 9.4  Backward compatibility.\n\n       Trivially preserved (additive only).\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 10. DEPENDENCIES & TOOLING\n================================================================\n\n\u00a7 10.1 New dependencies introduced by S8:\n\n       Production (runtime): NONE.\n\n       Dev / CI:\n       - `playwright` (Python bindings) \u2014 for e2e tests.\n         pip install playwright; playwright install chromium.\n\n\u00a7 10.2 Configuration files added:\n       - tests/e2e/playwright.config.json \u2014 minimal config\n         (chromium-only, headless by default, 30s timeout).\n\n\u00a7 10.3 New env vars:\n       - PLAYWRIGHT_HEADLESS (default: true). Set to false in\n         local dev for visual browser debug.\n\n\u00a7 10.4 No new production env vars.\n\n================================================================\n\u00a7 11.
